title: Durable model setup acceptance and concrete Auto selection preparation
tool: Native ownership source, interrupted-state fixtures, entry checks and prior CI receipts
---

Explicitly completed model setup now records acceptance under the inference owner's existing exclusive durable journal. Permission binds the compiled manifest, startup policy ID and full recipe identity. It is independent of authentication and a successful health check: acceptance never replaces the active receipt, clears a failed activation or marks an unverified runtime healthy. Duplicate acceptance preserves exact state-file bytes. Unsupported prior versions remain in the bounded history and cannot authorize current content. The original-only state schema remains readable with an absent acceptance list.

The concrete inference owner receives the completed setup object, checks its readiness and exact compiled pack/directory ownership, and requires an unloaded maintenance boundary. Failed acceptance persistence leaves a configuration failure rather than allowing queued work to proceed. The outer runtime marks settings pending before releasing setup maintenance. The UI permits its post-setup retry only after acceptance succeeds; file verification alone cannot clear an acceptance failure. Model files and user data are not deleted or moved by these operations.

At configuration or idle loading, LocalInference now builds the real Auto context from accepted versions whose declared files are present, separate per-pack component/storage observations, the saved memory preference and current headroom. The actual load still reauthenticates files, replans from fresh availability, checks health and commits before publication. Older successful original-only use has a narrow migration path; finding arbitrary other files grants no acceptance. The selected pack remains frozen through a pending attempt and rollback. Readiness and an inactive custom value do not trigger a new selection. The native telemetry stores the resulting selection reason, derives its displayed range and recommendation from the selected pack, and uses the full recipe in its range cache key. The outer runtime no longer imposes the original pack's range before the concrete owner can select its pack; default nonlocal owners retain the prior validation implementation.

Activation selections now additionally bind full recipe identity. Old records missing that field map to an independently frozen historical original recipe digest rather than whatever the current defaults happen to be. This closes same-name recipe changes. A previously used automatic version that no longer matches requires a new explicit verified setup rather than silently taking the fallback's new defaults. The exact historical JSON and derived digest are captured, with a native comparison fixture prepared. This is a compatibility identity, not a performance measurement.

New application fixtures cover offline permission persistence, exact idempotence, unknown versions, changed manifests and recipes, malformed/oversized/duplicate state, every atomic-write fault point, refusal of unchecked setup, maintenance ownership and queued-work configuration handoff. The existing pure recipe fixture checks the historical mapping. Thirty-two entry-point checks pass locally and the diff is clean. No compilation, tensor scan or model process ran for this stage. Native compilation, these added fixtures and a full real setup/activation drill remain pending.

The preceding proposal source at 9b91dcfa67306ab63b2d38fc554056cbd4bde5a9 passes its complete instrumented catalogue, including seventy-eight Auto-policy, ninety-two startup-default and thirty-five startup-selection assertions. Its external-library job and complete Mac/Xcode workflow pass. The captured full engine snapshot still has the weights-free job running. These receipts do not certify the current acceptance changes. The held-out continuation has completed job twenty-five and is running job twenty-six; no partial answers, grades or aggregate outcome were read. Alternative setup offers, the qualified standalone loader/distribution, actual profile population and confirmation against the loaded configuration, final quality/speed campaigns and integrated acceptance remain open. No alternative pack or profile is registered.
